The cultural development programme we plan and implement next semester consists of seven strategic areas. One is to rehabilitate the historical fabric of the city. In this regard, we have made our meetings with both our Ministry of Environment and Urbanism and the Historical Cities Association and the Association of Turkey's municipalities and relevant organisations, accelerating our work on the origins of the city's historical fabric. The second is the revitalization of cultural areas. Third, the enrichment and promotion of urban culture. The fourth is to uncover the traces of Anatolian civilization. The fifth is the support of culture economics and entrepreneurship. The sixth is the development of the cultural sponsorship system. The seventh is the strengthening of the human capacity and training of it. In these seven strategic areas, it is of great importance to revitalize the culture of family, neighborhood and co-existence, to encourage and participate in culture and art of citizens, to preserve and live in a holistic structure of cities yesterday and today, to update city memory, to preserve and preserve our cities' culture and art so that our cities don't become rural, and to update the memory and identity of our cities.
